\section*{Simplify deltas:}
\begin{subequations}\begin{gather}
T_{\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}\delta{}\epsilon{}\zeta{}\eta{}} \Omega{}^{A}_{\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}} \Phi{}^{B}_{\delta{}\epsilon{}\zeta{}\eta{}} = R_{z}^{-15} \cdot - \frac{1}{1575} \cdot  \left[ \begin{array}{c}

 \\[0.2em] 		 + \frac{36}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{yzz} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{yz\alpha{}\alpha{}}+ \frac{108}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{z\alpha{}\beta{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{zz\alpha{}\beta{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 + \frac{24}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{y\alpha{}\alpha{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{yzzz}+ \frac{12}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{y\beta{}\beta{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{yzzz} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{4}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{z\beta{}\gamma{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{\alpha{}\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{4}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{z\alpha{}\gamma{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{\alpha{}\beta{}\beta{}\gamma{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{4}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{z\alpha{}\beta{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}\gamma{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{8}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{y\alpha{}\alpha{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{yz\gamma{}\gamma{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{4}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{y\beta{}\beta{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{yz\gamma{}\gamma{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{8}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{z\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}}
 \end{array}\right] 
\\ \xRightarrow{\text{cleaned up}}
  \left[ \begin{array}{c} 
 		+ \frac{108}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{z\alpha{}\beta{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{zz\alpha{}\beta{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{4}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{z\beta{}\gamma{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{\alpha{}\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{4}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{z\alpha{}\gamma{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{\alpha{}\beta{}\beta{}\gamma{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{4}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{z\alpha{}\beta{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}\gamma{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{8}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{z\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}}
 \end{array}\right] 
\end{gather}\end{subequations}


\section*{Simplify Hexadecapoles:}

T_{\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}\delta{}\epsilon{}\zeta{}\eta{}} \Omega{}^{A}_{\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}} \Phi{}^{B}_{\delta{}\epsilon{}\zeta{}\eta{}} =  \left[ \begin{array}{c}

 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{8}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}^{A}_{\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}} \cdot \Phi{}^{B}_{z\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}}
 \end{array}\right]

 \end{array}\right]
